# Exposure ledger

Every model or tool touch (including a single-sample smoke) gets a row **before results are read**.
Exposure claims elsewhere must carry four fields: scope, date, counts, inherited exposure.

Field semantics (consolidated contract §6/§7; umbrella 续92/续93):

- `execution profile` — one of `model-free-check` / `baseline-reproduction` /
  `bounded-discovery-probe`; paper-scale profiles never appear in this ledger.
- `split role` — `discovery` or `dev`. `confirmatory` rows require Stage-3 paper authority
  (`OWNER_GO_AND_PAPER_EXECUTION_CONTRACT`) or a dated owner allowance and do not occur in Stage-2.
- `split identity hash` — SHA-256 over the exact sample-identity list this touch may read.
- `consumed` — `yes` only when confirmatory results were read; **irreversible once `yes`**;
  model-free and discovery/dev rows write `no`.
- `inherited exposure` — scoped statement of prior exposure carried into this touch;
  monotonically non-decreasing across rows.
- `calls` / `speech audio seconds` / `gpu hours` — the registered budget record for this
  run; every cell must be a **finite non-negative number** (the gate refuses NaN or
  negative cells outright — they would corrupt the arithmetic). These columns carry no
  numeric ceiling of their own (the first-slice caps that used to sum them as slice
  totals — consolidated contract §6 — were retired by the 2026-08-06 owner amendment,
  `SAEA-OWNER-AMENDMENT-2026-08-06-BUDGET-CAPS`): `contracts.FrozenCoreGate` no longer sums
  these columns across rows. What it still enforces, per row, is the cross-check that a
  plan may only claim what its OWN matched row registers: `registered ≥ planned` for each
  of the three columns, against the plan's own declared `planned_model_calls` /
  `planned_speech_audio_seconds` / `planned_gpu_hours`.

Inherited exposure baseline: program-level prior exposure (W1 probe campaigns on other carriers; no
Earnings21/Earnings22/ConEC model exposure inherited as of 2026-08-03). The carriers' only
processing so far is model-free acquisition/verification (D0).

The extension covers the sunk retired-protocol spend plus one complete segmented pass with retry and re-run headroom; per-row `registered ≥ planned` remains the only enforced cross-check (slice-sum caps were retired 2026-08-06). Attempt ledger at extension time: 8 attempts, 135 calls / 64,539 audio-s, all ABORTED except the session-teardown-killed dc5de3 (89 slices, no defect)** |

**2026-08-09 second amendment to the SAEA-E-002 rows (before any result is read).** The first launch of the `nocontext` arm ABORTED at slice 575/610 (attempt `attempt-20260809T090531Z-96ccb8`, 577 calls / 34,452 audio-s metered): the llama-server lost its CUDA context mid-run (process alive, 0 MiB GPU) and every later request hung to the 900 s transport ceiling; the bounded per-slice retry fired and also hit the dead server, so the attempt failed closed as designed. Cause: VRAM headroom — `-c 32768 -np 4` reserved 8k KV per slot against slices that need ~1.5k, leaving ~500 MB free on a 24 GB card. Fix attempt 1 (protocol-bound, new config hash): `-c 16384` (4k/slot). **That fix was itself WRONG and was stopped after 81 calls / 4,860 audio-s (attempt `attempt-20260809T112222Z-30ca91`, left OPENED when the operator killed it deliberately, before any result was read).** Reason for stopping: measurement showed the ConEC evidence materials the matched/mismatched arms inject run to 23.3 KB (~5,835 tokens; median 7.4 KB ≈ 1,850), so a worst-case evidence request needs ≈7,000 tokens — more than the 4,096/slot that `-c 16384 -np 4` provides. Letting it run would have completed the no-evidence arm and then failed the two evidence arms, invalidating the whole paired set (all three arms must share one config hash). A systematic memory strategy is being measured instead (KV quantization, CPU-RAM KV fallback using the 43 GB of idle system memory, partial layer offload, and a stable-prefix prompt ordering so a call's evidence block is prompt-cached once rather than re-sent per slice); all three arms then re-run together under one new hash. Both aborted/stopped attempts' spend stays on record here. **2026-08-09 third stop (before any result read): the first final-config attempt of the `nocontext` arm was killed at 86/610 slices after a 7.3× throughput collapse — first quarter 9.9 s/slice, last quarter 72.1 s/slice.** Cause: an ops agent's diagnostic process took a ~715 MiB CUDA context beside the run (it had assumed `-dev none` prevents a context; it does not), squeezing free VRAM to ~350 MiB; with `-ngl 999` disabling llama.cpp's device-memory fitting, allocations spill to host memory under pressure and are never migrated back, so throughput stayed degraded for 12+ minutes after the competing process died. Scientifically the outputs are unaffected (memory placement does not change arithmetic), but at 72 s/slice the three arms would have taken 30+ h instead of ~5 h, so the sequence was restarted on a verified-empty GPU (0 MiB, 0 compute apps). Standing rule adopted: **no process that can open a CUDA context may run beside a live experiment, diagnostics included.** **2026-08-09 fourth stop — a PROTOCOL DEFECT, not an environment one (before any result read):** the clean-GPU rerun of `nocontext` aborted at 581/610 with the same `[100054] asr … timed out`, and the earlier attempt aborted at 577 — a deterministic failure at ~578±3 on a specific input, with all 579 preceding slices healthy (15–16 s each, the last one 3.9 s). Root cause: **the model config declared no generation cap**, so a degenerate slice (silence/music/applause at a call's tail) can run away and generate until the 12,288-token slot fills — ≈600 s at ~20 tok/s — which exceeds even the 900 s transport ceiling, so the bounded per-slice retry could not help either (the retry would burn another 900 s the same way). Fix, protocol-bound and therefore a new config hash: `max_tokens: 1024` in `decoding_params` (≈4–5× the observed 146–250-token slice output, so legitimate transcripts are untouched) and `timeout_seconds` 900 → 240 (≈15× a healthy 16 s slice), which makes a runaway cost ~50 s and lets the retry actually recover. All three arms restart together under the new hash; every aborted attempt's spend stays on record above. **2026-08-09 correction to the fourth-stop diagnosis (recorded before the rerun's results are read).** Log forensics on `attempt-20260809T171603Z-1c09ee` confirmed part of the account above and falsified part of it. Confirmed: exactly one asr invocation never returned (580 `ability_start` events against 579 `ability_end`), and the arm aborted the instant that invocation's retry budget was exhausted. Wrong: it is **not** "a deterministic failure at ~578±3 on a specific input". The two aborted attempts hung on *different* samples (`4366429` in `96ccb8`, `4387383` in `1c09ee` — each attempt wrote 9 of the 10 per-sample derivation files, missing exactly the sample that hung), and the slice ordinal is incidental because `obs_batch_samples: 4` runs four samples concurrently. Nor was anything degrading: across all 580 requests the longest start→end gap was 38.6 s, and the run spanned 1.87 h, not the ~9 h a naive reading of the call count suggests. The fix stands and its rationale is strengthened: with no `max_tokens`, llama.cpp decodes until the slot's 12,288-token context fills, and because decoding is deterministic (temperature 0, fixed seed) the single bounded retry re-sent a byte-identical request and hung identically — **a bounded retry can only recover *transient* failures, never content-deterministic ones**, so capping generation is what actually makes the worst case finite. Consequence for reading the rerun: a slice whose completion is exactly 1024 tokens is a saturation flag rather than a transcript, and must be counted and reported, not scored silently.

**2026-08-09 amendment to all three SAEA-E-002 rows above (before any result is read).** The arms now run: (i) on the **INT4 model** `qwen3-omni-30b-a3b-instruct-gguf-q4km` (model fragment `qwen3-omni-llamacpp-q4km`, runtime receipt `docs/receipts/runtime-q4km.json` sha256 `7be32a78…`) — same switch, same justification and declared limitation as the SAEA-E-001 row; (ii) under the **obs-agent-loop protocol on the openJiuwen batch executor** (baselines `bare-core-ojw` / `fixed-legal-context-ojw` / `mismatched-evidence-ojw`, `obs_batch_samples=4`), so a call is many 60 s slice requests rather than one whole-call request; (iii) on the **mini sample** `r4-e21-sub10` — a seeded 10-of-44 subset of the frozen e21 discovery split, receipt `docs/receipts/probe-samples/r4-e21-sub10-sample.json`, bound into `configs/probes/manifest.json` under the sample-once policy (the rows' split identity hash is the MINI subset's run-identity hash 744c4b2e2d2eece3b05c68089c02000e38c96d5c62db8055d9c421fd509a8f61, because the gate requires the row to name exactly the sample set the plan will read; the parent 44-id discovery split fcc1b0bc55432d02347dcdf730c6bcef561a9b0ad9605c45b367e1ed3da5d69e remains the provenance of that subset and is recorded here in prose. The first chained R4 launch was REFUSED by contracts.gate_exposure on exactly this mismatch, before any model touch). Budget cells raised to the same 2000 / 150000 / 30.0 envelope as SAEA-E-001 to cover slice-level metering; the pre-registered success criterion is unchanged.

The open question this settles is whether the CPU encode OVERLAPS GPU decode (then B/C win despite the 2x) or serialises (then B loses ~8%). Registration note: the first write of this row carried the receipt's own `split_identity_hash` field, and the gate refused the touch because the execution plan binds a DIFFERENT hash — `reproduction.probe_split` computes two, a receipt-integrity hash over the stored item keys and `VerifiedSplit.identity_hash` over the carrier-prefixed ids, and only the latter is what a plan and therefore a ledger row must carry (the same distinction the already-registered SAEA-E-002 rows show: receipt `fa1d2982…` versus row `744c4b2e…`). Corrected before any model touch occurred; the gate cost nothing but a one-minute refusal. |

(a model touch additionally requires the runtime receipt and a validated `ExecutionPlan`
whose `run/experiment id` is pre-registered above, per the consolidated execution contract)

**2026-08-10 serving-throughput probe (pre-registered BEFORE any result is read).** Owner-directed infrastructure measurement, not an experiment: no scientific claim is derived from it, no split is scored, and no transcript is read. The first persisted llama-server log (`SAEA-E-002-r4-matched` attempt `72179f`) shows generation at **3.07–4.40 tokens/s per slot, ≈16 tokens/s aggregate**, against 470 tokens/s prefill — a prefill:decode ratio near 138:1 where 10–30:1 is normal, on a 3B-active MoE at Q4_K_M. The same log shows llama.cpp refusing to size the model to the device: `common_fit_params: failed to fit params to free device memory: n_gpu_layers already set by user to 999, abort`, with 23,406 of 24,462 MiB resident (95.7 %) and the server's own prompt cache climbing past 1,559 MiB against an 8,192 MiB limit. Hypothesis under test (owner's): decode is memory-bandwidth-bound, so any spill of weights or KV to host memory collapses it — the same mechanism already recorded here on 2026-08-09, when one competing 715 MiB CUDA context cost 7.3× and did not recover. **Probe:** four serving configurations — (A) current `-c 49152 -np 4 -fa on -ngl 999 -ctk q8_0 -ctv q8_0`; (B) A with `--no-mmproj-offload`, which returns the mmproj's 2,940 MiB worst case to host memory; (C) f16 KV at `-c 32768`; (D) B+C combined — each serving a handful of identical requests built from ONE already-derived discovery slice (`derived/obs-slices/…/4330115/i0000.wav`), measured on the server's own reported prefill/decode timings. Discovery split only; no confirmatory identity is touched; the probe reads throughput numbers and VRAM, never transcript quality. **The R4 sequence was deliberately stopped mid-`matched` to free the GPU for this**, so that arm's partial spend stands on record and all three arms re-run afterwards under whichever configuration wins — serving args are inside the config hash, so this necessarily invalidates the already-completed `nocontext` attempt for pairing purposes and it re-runs too.

**2026-08-10 SAEA-BENCH-serving-q4km read-out (results read this afternoon; row `consumed` stays `no` per §7 — discovery role, no confirmatory content).** Three attempts COMPLETED at study commit `fb234a8`, one per configuration, and the wall-clock numbers plus transcripts were read: **(A) `qwen3-omni-llamacpp-q4km` mmproj-GPU `-np 4`: 22.8 min net wall, 10.8 s/slice, mean WER 0.2359 (attempt `fd7ba3`, 126 calls, peak VRAM 23,402 MiB); (B) mmproj-CPU: 29.1 min, 14.0 s/slice, WER 0.2601 (`21a665`, 125 calls, 20,416 MiB); (C) mmproj-CPU `-np 8`: 31.0 min, 14.9 s/slice, WER 0.3451 with 1 saturated runaway slice on `4387383` (`230144`, 125 calls).** The pre-registered prediction resolved: CPU audio-encode does NOT overlap GPU decode — B's +3.2 s/slice equals the full measured encode delta, ~28% of net wall on this workload (the registered "~8%" was long-prompt-specific). **Verdict: configuration A is LOCKED as the deployment configuration; B and C are retired and their fragments `configs/model/q4km-mmprojcpu.json` / `q4km-mmprojcpu-np8.json` deleted from the working tree (bytes preserved at `fb234a8`); reintroduction requires a new registered bench.** The registered correctness expectation (identical WER) was FALSIFIED: serving configuration perturbs numerics — per-sample WER moves ±3–7 pp with mmproj placement and slot count, noise-like in direction, and the perturbation propagates into segmentation (slice counts 126/125/125 on identical audio), so serving args are confirmed load-bearing inside the config hash and A's arguments may not change mid-campaign without a new bench. A fourth attempt (`639d36`, config A, determinism repeat 1 of a planned same-config byte-compare pair) ABORTED before its first request — the freshly-loaded llama-server process died and the session layer refused fail-closed (`pid … is not a live process`), 0 calls / 0 audio-s metered — so run-to-run determinism under the locked configuration remains OPEN; the repeats stay inside this row's registered envelope (376 of 1,500 calls, 22,340 of 60,000 audio-s consumed). Cumulative spend on this row: 376 calls / 22,340 audio-s / ~0.94 gpu-h (gpu_seconds 1,190+1,081+1,123). Full read-out: `docs/readiness/2026-08-10-serving-config-verdict.md`.

**2026-08-11 determinism repeat read-out (SAEA-BENCH-serving-q4km, results read before anything further launched).** Attempt `54a5e7` COMPLETED at study commit `142c33f` under config hash `dc67f633…` — byte-identical configuration to attempt `fd7ba3` (same serving args, same seed, temperature 0). **Verdict: NON-DETERMINISTIC.** All three transcripts diverge; 114 of 129 shared slice responses differ, the first at slice 3 of sample 1; slice counts differ (126 vs 124) because a flipped token near a model-declared boundary moves the cut and shifts every later slice — the segmentation cascade, same numerics family as the serving bench's C-config divergence. Magnitude: mean WER 0.2359 → 0.2680 (**±3.2 pp run-to-run on the 3-sample mean**); per-sample −3.5 / +3.6 / **+9.6 pp**; wall clock stable (~24.9 min vs 24.1); completion tokens 19,255 vs 19,827. Binding consequence, effective immediately: **single-run paired deltas below ~3 pp (3-sample mean) or ~10 pp (per-sample) are unreadable at this protocol**; the E-002F rows below carry that proviso, and the full-R4 noise treatment (more samples, repeated runs, or `-np 1` composition-invariant serving at est. ~1.7× wall) is an owner decision queued behind the probe ladder (`docs/readiness/2026-08-11-segmentation-efficiency-frontier.md`). Secondary finding from the same run's exit code: `run_arm_sequence.sh` never passed `--ledger-cells`, so every COMPLETED arm it ever launched (bench attempts `fd7ba3`/`21a665`/`230144`, this repeat) finished its science, then failed `finalize_bundle`'s draft-ledger-row rendering and stayed OPENED — never FINALIZED. Launcher fixed in this commit (explicit PENDING draft cells); the affected attempts' manifests/scores/traces are verified and unaffected; their attempt states remain OPENED on record. This repeat's spend joins the row's total: cumulative now 500 calls / 29,776 audio-s / ~1.27 gpu-h of the registered 1,500 / 60,000 / 10.0.

**2026-08-11 E-002F fast-set read-out (all three rows above; results read).** `nocontext` COMPLETED+FINALIZED (attempt `dfc630`, 126 calls / 7,451 audio-s, mean WER **0.2604**, per-sample 0.2890/0.2670/0.2251) — as pre-registered it is the THIRD observation of the same-configuration noise distribution (config hash `dc67f633…` shared with bench `fd7ba3` 0.2359 and repeat `54a5e7` 0.2680): three-run mean band **[0.2359, 0.2680]**, per-sample range up to **13.6 pp** (4366522: 0.153/0.249/0.289) with per-sample orderings inverting between runs. `mismatched` COMPLETED+FINALIZED (attempt `5458ce`, 132 calls / 7,815 audio-s, mean WER **0.2623**, 0 saturated). `matched` **ABORTED at 107 calls / 6,358 audio-s** (attempt `96e540`): sample 4387383 crossed `obs_slice_skip_max=1` after two slices near ordinal i0026 exhausted their retry — and forensics shows the four failing requests **never reached the server and were never metered** (server completed 107 = calls_used 107; zero `-r1` request ids anywhere; slice wavs beyond i0025 never written): a deterministic BUILD-TIME failure whose actual exception was swallowed unlogged by `request_slice_with_retry` and lost with the unpersisted trace of an ABORTED attempt — the same same-sample slices passed in the `nocontext` arm's different cascade path the same night, so the failure is evidence-arm/path-dependent. Diagnosability defect fixed in this commit (one WARNING per swallowed retryable failure, class+message+attempt id; zero semantic change); `matched` re-runs under the same row (spend to date stays on record; registered envelope holds 1,500 calls). Reading of the pre-registered criterion: NOT EVALUABLE this pass (matched missing); the readable fragment — nocontext 0.2604 vs mismatched 0.2623, Δ 0.2 pp — is far below the ±3.2 pp noise floor and is recorded as unreadable exactly as the rows' proviso anticipated.

**2026-08-11 SAEA-BENCH-slice10-q4km read-out (results read; row `consumed` stays `no`, discovery role).** Attempt `f8292e` COMPLETED+FINALIZED at study commit `dbd97fe`, config hash `26267d2f…`: **749 calls / 7,485 audio-s / 42 min wall (gate 82 s), mean WER 0.5337** (0.4389 / **0.7708** / 0.3914), 3 saturated slices, prompt tokens **263,439 total (2.11× the 60 s run's 124,903 — the cost model's 2.14× prediction verified to 1.4%)**, mean 352 prompt-tok and 42.8 completion-tok per slice, per-slice latency mean/median/p90 = 8.36/8.03/10.23 s. **Both pre-registered kill-rules fired**: net wall 40.6 min ≥ 22.8 min (prediction ii — the per-call floor dominates) AND WER degraded +26.6 pp above the three-run noise band (prediction iii — the stitch mechanism scales with seam count); prediction i (decode-context) is REFUTED as the governing effect at this granularity. New constants extracted for the frontier: two-point fit `latency ≈ 0.40×audio_seconds + 4.3 s` — a **≈4.3 s per-call floor** invisible in the 13–60 s fit range; and a **~18 completion-token per-call output intercept** (boundary-report format), 40% of a 10 s slice's output. Standing consequence (per the pre-registered decision rules, mechanical): **10 s overlapping slicing is disqualified for scientific arms on both axes; the efficiency direction is locked to fewer/longer calls (S1/S2) plus cache affinity (S5); short steps remain viable only inside seamless (S4) designs, now with a measured floor to design against.** Fragment `a2a-buffer10-ojw` is retained as a measured reference point (it is an explicit per-arm choice, not a serving-default hazard). Spend: this row cumulative 749 calls / 7,485 audio-s / ~0.59 gpu-h of 1,500 / 60,000 / 10.0.

**2026-08-11 matched re-run read-out — the fast set is complete (results read).** Attempt `d02706` COMPLETED+FINALIZED at study commit `b92fded`: 133 calls / 7,884 audio-s, mean WER **0.2513** (0.2241 / 0.2298 / 0.3000), 0 saturated, 0 skipped — and **zero `failed retryably` warnings** from the newly armed logging: the build-time failure that killed attempt `96e540` did NOT recur on this cascade path. Its cause therefore remains unidentified and path-dependent; the logging stays armed, and the next recurrence will name its class and message in the driver log. **Paired reading of the pre-registered criterion (single runs, judged against the ±3.2 pp noise floor):** matched 0.2513 < nocontext 0.2604 < mismatched 0.2623 — the full monotone ordering matches the context-dose hypothesis in DIRECTION, but the separations (0.9–1.1 pp on the 3-sample mean) sit well inside the noise floor and the per-sample paired deltas swing −6.5 to +7.5 pp with mixed signs. Recorded verdict: **directionally consistent, not readable as signal** — exactly the outcome class the rows' proviso pre-registered. Iteration steer (what the fast split exists for): do not spend further n=3 passes; the next context-dose evidence must come from `r4-e21-sub10` (n=10) under a noise treatment — repeated runs or `-np 1` composition-invariant serving — and, if adopted after its bench, id_slot cache affinity for the evidence arms (frontier P2). Spend: the matched row now totals 240 calls / 14,242 audio-s of its registered 1,500 / 60,000 / 10.0; GPU idle from 05:25Z.

On the bench's own question the verdict is decisive: **1 prefix-cache hit in 91 evidence slices under pinning alone** — pinning works (the one hit re-prefilled exactly tail+audio, ~799 tok) but cannot help while the per-slice rolling tail sits in the SYSTEM message ahead of the stable evidence block (`ojw/components.py` appends the tail to the instruction), so the cacheable prefix dies before the evidence starts. **Adoption rule outcome: FAILED on wall (pace ≈ the OFF reference until the abort); fragment `q4km-slotaffinity` is retired-superseded, not deleted — its id_slot mechanism carries forward into the combined bench registered below.** Spend on this row: 95 calls / 5,645 audio-s / ~0.5 gpu-h of 1,500 / 60,000 / 10.0.

**2026-08-11 SAEA-BENCH-featcache correction (before any score was read).** The first COLD attempt was deliberately stopped at 52 slices and is INVALID: the pre-registered "cold run must show no hits" reading caught **382 false cache hits** — `mtmd_input_chunk_get_id` returns the BITMAP-level hash, shared by every ~104-token chunk cut from one audio buffer (~7.4 chunks per 60 s slice), so after the first chunk back-filled, every later chunk of the same slice "hit" and was injected with the FIRST chunk's features. The poisoned cache directory was purged; the key now includes the chunk's prompt position (patched build `862effd0f`, receipt regenerated, diff refreshed at docs/patches/2026-08-11-mtmd-feature-cache.diff; reuse contract unchanged for identical prompts — cold/warm, same-arm repeats — and cross-arm position drift deliberately misses, media-ordinal refinement deferred). The aborted attempt's spend (~52 calls / ~3,100 audio-s) stands on the row; the cold/warm pair restarts under the fixed build.

**2026-08-11 SAEA-BENCH-featcache cold/warm read-out — benefit NOT yet realized; both causes located (results read; `consumed` stays `no`).** COLD attempt `2afd8f` COMPLETED: 19.4 min / 102 calls / WER 0.2310 / **0 hits** (key fix verified) / 806 features back-filled — and vs the unpatched fixedwindow reference (2a0d08: 20.0 min / 0.2316) the patch's cold overhead is ~zero. WARM attempt `0b7e9f` COMPLETED: **20.1 min (no wall gain), 483 hits / 323 encodes (60% hit rate), WER 0.2461, transcripts not byte-identical to cold.** Causes, read directly from the numbers: (1) **position-keyed cache entries break under tail-length drift** — the rolling tail's token count varies with transcript noise across runs, shifting every audio chunk's prompt position (fixed windows removed the AUDIO cascade; the tail still carries a TEXT cascade) — fix: key by media ordinal within the bitmap, invariant to text prefix length; (2) **the 9p mount tax** — features on /mnt/e cost ~per-hit reads that roughly cancel the encode savings — fix: cache dir moves to ext4 (mlruns precedent for performance-critical local state). Honest revision on record: the recoverable ceiling on this arm is 10–29% of wall, pending clean next-round data on how serial encode truly is under 4-way batching. Verdict: NOT lockable; one fix round (ordinal keys + ext4 dir, new build/receipt/config hash) re-runs the pair. Spend added: ~204 calls / ~12.1k audio-s across the two attempts (plus the earlier invalidated 52-call cold).

**2026-08-11 SAEA-BENCH-featcache fix round 2 (before any result read).** Build `1c806481c` (receipt regenerated, diff refreshed): cache keys switch from prompt position to **media ordinal within the bitmap** — invariant to the rolling tail's token-length drift that capped round 1 at 60% hits; and the cache directory moves to **ext4** (`/home/chao/feat-cache/q4km`, config-hashed via server_env) — the 9p mount tax on /mnt/e reads roughly cancelled round 1's hit savings (mlruns precedent: performance-critical local state on ext4; the FEATURES remain GC-able cache, not governed corpus bytes — the derivation chain lives in the run artifacts). Round-3 pre-registered expectations: warm hits = 100%, warm encode lines = 0, and the warm-vs-cold wall delta becomes the CLEAN measured value of the recoverable encode share — the lock-decision number. Cold/warm pair re-runs under the same row.

**2026-08-11 SAEA-BENCH-featcache round-3 read-out — FAILED, bench HELD (results read; `consumed` stays `no`).** Cold `99bdf1`: 19.0 min, **302 hits where zero were possible** (empty ext4 dir at launch), 504 encodes, WER **0.5740**; warm `f7a0eb`: 18.3 min, 806 hits / 0 encodes, WER **0.5725**, not byte-identical. Both transcripts are garbage: the ordinal keys collide in a THIRD, not-yet-understood way (~3 false hits per slice vs round 1's ~7.4 — the differing ratios prove both of my reverse-engineered models of the server's chunk microstructure are incomplete). Per the owner's rule — no lock without a passing measured number — and this study's own discipline: **the feature-cache line STOPS here for redesign rather than a third blind patch. Config A′ on the unpatched pinned build remains the locked deployment baseline (its −37% verdict and all rails are unaffected).** Rework requirements recorded for the next design round: (i) single-request instrumentation forensics of the actual chunk/id/ordinal structure BEFORE any key design; (ii) a self-validating cache mode (sampled hit-vs-encode byte comparison that poisons-and-refuses on mismatch) as a permanent rail, not a debug aid — this round proved silent feature corruption produces plausible-looking runs whose WER alone reveals the damage. Spend added: ~204 calls / ~12.1k audio-s. The wave1-sub5 A-1a confirmation and the R4 n=10 resume proceed under A′.

**2026-08-11 SAEA-E-004 A-1a confirmation read-out (results read; rows stay `consumed=no`).** Both arms COMPLETED+FINALIZED on the previously unexposed wave1-e21-sub5 design split under locked A′: control (model boundary) mean WER 0.2282 / 273 calls / 16,209 audio-s; fixedwindow **0.2166 / 226 calls / 13,467 audio-s**. **The pre-registered adoption rule FIRES: 4 of 5 samples improve (−5.7/−1.6/+7.5/−2.4/−3.7 pp) and corpus WER is better (−1.2 pp) → S1 (fixed windows) becomes the default substrate for NEW arms via a re-registered baseline set.** Honest calibration on record: the n=5 mean delta (−1.2 pp) is far below the fast pair's −6.7 (fast-split bias evident) and sits inside single-run noise — the CONSERVATIVE reading is "WER at least no worse"; adoption stands on the noise-free structural gains (−17% calls and audio, run-invariant slice bytes → no boundary cascade, feature-cache premise) plus the boundary machinery's independently measured defects. Running/registered experiments keep their obs-loop design; the S1 baseline set re-registration follows the R4 read-out. Spend: 499 calls / 29,676 audio-s across the two rows.

**2026-08-11 SAEA-E-002 rows, THIRD budget amendment (before any result read; precedent: the 2026-08-08 second extension).** Tonight's resume aborted in 8 minutes with the armed logging naming the cause exactly: `call budget exhausted: plan SAEA-E-002-r4-nocontext allows 2000 calls (1994 already spent by other attempts on this run)` — the per-row CROSS-ATTEMPT meter has absorbed every prior generation on these rows (the retired whole-call era, the three 2026-08-09 CUDA/config-era aborted attempts at 577/81/86 calls, the fourth-stop 581, the completed 63d0f57-era 614-call pass, and the operator-stopped 46-call resume), leaving 6 calls of headroom — the meter did precisely its job: force explicit accounting before more spend. **Amendment: all three rows' registered cells rise 2000→3500 calls / 150,000→250,000 audio-s / 30→45 gpu-h**, sized for one full three-arm A′-era pass (~620 calls/arm + retry headroom) on top of the recorded sunk spend; per-row `registered ≥ planned` remains the only enforced cross-check. The 8-minute aborted attempt (6 calls) and the 46-call operator stop stand on the record. The three arms relaunch as one sequence.

**2026-08-12 SAEA-BENCH-featcache FORENSICS CLOSED — the collision was one off-by-one (no model touch; log + source forensics only).** Round-3's structure, read from the cold log: each 60 s slice is EIGHT ~104-token media chunks; per request exactly 5 encodes + 3 false hits at the even ordinals. Root cause, verified against source: `find_next_media_chunk` is `upper_bound` (STRICTLY greater), and the ordinal scan advanced by `start + n_tokens` — landing exactly ON the next chunk's start and skipping it, so even ordinals collapsed onto the preceding odd ones and o2/o4/o6 were injected with o1/o3/o5's features (~26 s of each slice heard the wrong audio → WER 0.57). Round-1's ~7.4 false hits/slice (bitmap-only keys, 8 chunks one key) closes under the same model. **Fix build `5d9dfcb58`** (receipt updated in this commit): the one-line advance fix plus a VALIDATE mode (`LLAMA_MTMD_FEAT_CACHE_VALIDATE=1`, fragment q4km-featcache-validate): the hit short-circuit is disabled, everything encodes, and every re-encountered key is byte-compared against the cache — a mismatch logs loudly and is overwritten with encoder truth. **Endgame trio pre-registered under this row:** VALIDATE-1 (populate + intra-run self-check), VALIDATE-2 (full-coverage compare; **806/806 MATCH and 0 MISMATCH is the constructive proof that the cache serves correct features — the owner's "WER 对齐" question answered by construction**), then a clean warm (validation off, full hits) whose wall against the cold reference is **the measured efficiency gain under correct supply — the owner's question 2**. WER of every run is additionally judged against the fixedwindow reference 0.2316/0.2310.

**2026-08-12 SAEA-E-002 R4 n=10 read-out — the paired criterion is NOT met, and the negative control earned its keep (results read; discovery rows stay `consumed=no`; analysis JSON at docs/checks/speech-aware-evidence-acquisition/r4-context-dose-comparison.json).** All three arms COMPLETED+FINALIZED under locked A′ in one sequence (613/608/611 calls; saturated-and-quarantined 2/2/5). Corpus WER: nocontext **27.12%**, matched **24.36%**, mismatched **24.60%** (macro 25.93/24.21/24.54). Pre-registered contrasts (exact Wilcoxon two-sided + 10k-resample bootstrap, seed 20260807): matched−nocontext mean **−1.7 pp**, CI95 [−4.5, +1.0], p=0.375 — direction holds, not significant; **matched−mismatched −0.33 pp, CI [−1.2, +0.5], p=0.557 — NO separation from the negative control**; mismatched−nocontext −1.4 pp, CI crosses zero. **Verdict: the success criterion (matched < nocontext AND matched < mismatched) FAILS on its second leg. The ~1.5 pp benefit of a context block is CONTENT-INDEPENDENT at this dose/form — deranged other-call ConEC lists help as much as the right call's lists.** This is the outcome the design's own caveat anticipated (ConEC materials are unigram frequency bags, and a deranged bag still carries generic earnings-domain vocabulary): the measured value is DOMAIN PRIMING, not call-specific facts. Steers, recorded: (i) E-003/B-2a's form contrasts gain priority (does form unlock call-specific value?); (ii) the self-supplied glossary path (L3) sharpens — if generic domain text is worth ~1.5 pp, a call's OWN accumulating vocabulary may be worth more at zero leakage; (iii) any claim about "evidence quality" on this carrier must first beat the domain-priming floor the mismatched arm now defines. Spend: 1,832 calls / 109,233 audio-s / ~7.5 gpu-h across the three rows this pass.

**2026-08-12 SAEA-BENCH-featcache ENDGAME read-out — correctness PROVEN, efficiency NULL; retired with the hypothesis honestly refuted (results read; `consumed` stays `no`).** Trio under the fixed build `5d9dfcb58`: VALIDATE-1 `148c09` (20.1 min, 806 encodes, 0 hits, WER 0.2229 — cache populated cleanly); VALIDATE-2 `70aee2` (19.8 min, 806 encodes, 0 hits, WER 0.2406 — **wiring hole recorded honestly: the validate compare checks only the process-local RAM layer, and a fresh server starts empty, so 0 comparisons fired across processes**; correctness is instead established by the stronger evidence below); CLEAN WARM `576996`: **806/806 hits, 0 encodes — the encoder fully bypassed — WER 0.2326, mid-band among the four no-cache fixedwindow runs (0.2229–0.2406): correct feature supply CONFIRMED by WER equivalence. Wall 19.7 min vs 19.0–20.1 across cold/reference runs: the efficiency gain is ZERO.** Secondary metrics: gpu_seconds 919/899→859 (−5%) and latency_total −6% — the skipped encode shows up per-request but the 4-way schedule absorbs it entirely; decode saturates the device and inline GPU encode was never on the critical path (the serving bench's +3.2 s/slice was the CPU-encode stall, a different mechanism). **Verdict: the feature-cache line closes — the owner's question 1 answered (off-by-one ordinal collapse fed wrong features), question 2 answered by the cleanest possible measurement (with correct supply, ~0 wall gain at -np 4 on this workload). The patched build, receipts, and diff stay archived as engineering record; no re-lock; A′ remains the deployment baseline.** Cumulative featcache spend: ~816 calls / ~48.6k audio-s / ~3.5 gpu-h across seven attempts — the price of replacing two plausible estimates (30%, then 10–29%) with one measured zero.

**2026-08-12 SAEA-E-004F-a1a-notail read-out — the tail was actively POISONING transcription (results read; `consumed` stays `no`).** Attempt `fac835` COMPLETED+FINALIZED, 19 min / 102 calls / 0 saturated: **mean WER 0.1409** against the fixedwindow tail=400 reference band **[0.2229, 0.2406]** — not merely in-band but **8–10 pp BETTER**, every sample improving massively (0.1073 vs 0.2309; 0.1072 vs 0.2222; 0.2082 vs 0.2418), a margin ~3× the run-to-run noise band. The rolling tail — the model's own prior output fed back as conditioning context, a Whisper-style carry-over from the overlap/stitch era — was **feeding the model's own errors forward**: the error self-reinforcement mechanism this study's thesis worries about in evidence form, resident in our own substrate at a ~9 pp cost for the entire experimental history. Cascade of consequences, all positive: (i) the bare-core floor on this split drops from ~0.23 to **~0.14** — larger than every evidence-injection gain measured to date (R4's context benefit was 1.5 pp); (ii) the LAST cross-slice dependency is gone — slice-level batch scheduling is unlocked (effective concurrency = min(np, total slices); fast-3's ~102 slices can saturate np8; straggler idle eliminated; uniform batch shapes); (iii) with no cross-slice dependency of any kind, byte-level determinism becomes plausible again; (iv) retrospective: the cache-key tail-drift breakage, the text cascade in the noise mechanism, and the sample-level serialization were all SYMPTOMS of the tail. Directional (n=3, single run, fast-split bias caveat as ever); the wave1-sub5 confirmation + a same-config repeat (determinism byte-compare, free) is the natural next pair before any baseline re-registration builds on this. Spend: 102 calls / 6,035 audio-s / ~0.4 gpu-h.

**2026-08-12 SAEA-BENCH-slicebatch first pair: refused fail-closed in 3 minutes each — the adapter caught the executor gap (before any result read).** Both runs aborted with the armed logging naming it exactly: `id_slot must be a non-negative integer, got 'per-sample'` — the locked A′ sentinel is resolved by the OJW executor, and the slice-parallel path runs the PLAIN policy, which did not resolve it. Fix in this commit: the plain parallel path resolves the sentinel per DISPATCH SLOT (a pool of obs_batch_slices integers, acquired per request, released on settle — the ojw free-slots pattern; per-sample pinning is meaningless when one sample spans all slots). Spend: ~8 refused-unmetered attempts, ~0 calls. The pair relaunches.

The first pair of this row (refused on the sentinel) and the session-killed third attempt stand on the record. Consequence chain: the notail request is ~850 tokens, so -np 8 needs only 2048 ctx/slot (-c 16384, SMALLER KV than A′) and fits with mmproj resident — step 3 (np8) is registered below and runs immediately. Spend this row: ~204 calls / ~12.1k audio-s.

**2026-08-16 P2 R-ladder block ABORTED AT MANIFEST BIND; the r0bias attempt is sealed unread.** The five-arm block launched 2026-08-16T00:21Z under the four rows above (pre-registration committed `a001a22`) did not fail in science - it failed at manifest bind. The English-only rewrite of `AGENTS.md`/`CLAUDE.md` landed in the working tree mid-flight (file mtime 2026-08-16T01:13:40Z) and `core/bundle/git_commit.py` refuses to bind `study_commit` against a dirty tree, so no COMPLETED manifest could be built. Arm `SAEA-P2-r0bias`, attempt `attempt-20260816T002109Z-4e1f59`, had by then RUN ITS INFERENCE TO COMPLETION - 156 min, **395 metered model calls, 30959.04 speech-audio seconds** (405 MODEL_REQUEST trace records less the 10 terminal stitch requests), all ten replay derivations plus trace, outputs, trace-manifest and server log written - yet its manifest was finalized `status: ABORTED` with `failure_reason` "run succeeded but its COMPLETED manifest could not be built: ... uncommitted changes". Arm `SAEA-P2-r1verify` (attempt `attempt-20260816T025734Z-23a43f`) died identically ~13 min in, and the driver's two-failure rule stopped the sequence at 03:10Z. **No results were read at abort time; `consumed` stays `no`.** The r0bias raw trace is durable and sealed, and its results will be read EXACTLY ONCE from that attempt via offline salvage; the arm is therefore NOT re-flown, because one-shot attempt semantics are preserved by reading the attempt that ran rather than flying a second one. This spend is recorded against the registered 2500 / 170000 / 20.0 cells of the `SAEA-P2-r0bias` row above.

**2026-08-16 four-arm resume - SAME pre-registration, no new registration.** The block resumes with the four un-run arms only - `SAEA-P2-r1verify`, `SAEA-P2-r2draft`, `SAEA-P2-r0wrong`, `SAEA-P2-r1wrong`, in that order - from `docs/arms/p2-rladder-resume.arms`, whose header is copied verbatim from `docs/arms/p2-rladder.arms`: the PRE-REGISTERED READS and the BRANCH RULES are unchanged, as are the split identity hash, the tier fields and the registered budget cells. The four rows above remain the governing registration; **no new registration was created and no new registration ID exists.** `consumed` stays `no` on all four. The resume is launched detached from Windows per plan rule I1, only after the working tree was made clean by the single commit carrying the guide translation, this resume arms file and these two notes - the I2 rule (no commits of any kind during any arm flight) governs the whole block.

**2026-08-16 the r0bias one-shot salvage read was SPENT (recorded after the fact, GPU-gap housekeeping).** The `SAEA-P2-r0bias` row above (and the abort note preceding this paragraph) pre-registered that the sealed `attempt-20260816T002109Z-4e1f59` trace "will be read EXACTLY ONCE" via offline salvage. That one-shot read **WAS SPENT 2026-08-16 06:48–07:18 local (EDT, UTC-04:00)**, and it happened **before** the HER/RIR definitions were pinned — disclosed in umbrella record `2026-08-16-p2-metric-definitions.md` §2 (mirrored in this repo at `docs/p2-metric-definitions-mirror.md`; renamed same-day from `2026-08-16-p2-metric-definition-amendment.md`, mirrored content-addressed against commit `34fb703` so the rename cannot break the reference). The read's tooling and output bytes are preserved durably at `docs/checks/speech-aware-evidence-acquisition/2026-08-16-p2-r0bias-salvage/` (commit `5f7ae83`); see that directory's own `README.md` for the full provenance and the withheld-bytes ledger.

Separately, for completeness of attempt accounting: arm `SAEA-P2-r1verify`, attempt `attempt-20260816T025734Z-23a43f` (22:57–23:10 EDT 2026-08-15), died at the same dirty-tree manifest-bind failure described above and **left NO terminal manifest** — unlike its two predecessors (the `SAEA-P2-r0bias` attempt above and the earlier serving-bench manifest-bind failures on record in this ledger), which each finalized with a terminal `ABORTED` manifest. CLAUSE MARGINS: SPEECH-TERM-PRESENT (I >= +0.06) NOT MET, margin -0.0137; KEY-ONLY CI-gated (entire 90 percent CI inside [-0.03, +0.03] AND reachable) NOT MET -- CI high +0.0970 overshoots the +0.03 ceiling by +0.0670 while CI low -0.0033 IS inside the band, so the clause failed on the CI alone and never on reachability; KEY-ONLY point-only pre-amendment reading (abs(I) <= 0.03) NOT MET, over by -0.0163 -- both KEY-ONLY readings AGREE here, the point estimate was already outside the equivalence band before the CI gate applied, so this is not a case of the amendment overturning a point-level pass; TEXT-DOMINANT (I <= -0.06) NOT MET, margin -0.1063. The estimate landed in the registered 0.03-wide DEAD ZONE between the KEY-ONLY ceiling (0.03) and the SPEECH-TERM-PRESENT floor (+0.06), at almost exactly its midpoint (+0.045): more samples cannot resolve this at ANY n, because KEY-ONLY can never fire while abs(I) > 0.03 and SPEECH-TERM-PRESENT can never fire while I < +0.06 -- only a MOVED estimate can. Recorded as a finding about the registered instrument; it is not a licence to re-cut thresholds after the fact and the read stands as registered. **PER-ARM** (official pooled SLU-F1, n=60 each): AUD-NOSUP 0.1741 / AUD-SUP 0.6138 / TXT-NOSUP 0.1740 / TXT-SUP 0.5674; Delta_AUD = +0.4397, Delta_TXT = +0.3934 -- roughly 89 percent of the supply benefit is realized text-side. slot_f1_exact 0.0435 / 0.5236 / 0.0339 / 0.3708; intent_acc 0.0000 on ALL FOUR arms (a PRE-EXISTING property of this scoring stack on this manifest, matching the consumed P-SLU parent read, not something this flight introduced); induced substitutions 0 / 3 / 0 / 3 (modality-symmetric, contributing nothing to the interaction); copy_rate 0.0000 on both supply arms across 6,000 supplied-item checks each. AUD-NOSUP's SLU-F1 and slot_f1_exact are BIT-IDENTICAL to the consumed P-SLU parent read's A0 arm (0.1740786454061426 / 0.043478260869565216) -- exact cross-session reproduction of the baseline a week apart, independent evidence that the frozen core plus this decode config is deterministic; AUD-SUP sits +0.0177 above that parent's A3 (0.5961), which is a prompt-content difference since AUD-SUP is registered as A3-FORM rather than A3-identical. **PERCEPTION GAP** (TXT-NOSUP - AUD-NOSUP) = **-0.0001** SLU-F1 (-0.0000863; -0.0096 on slot_f1_exact, about -22 percent relative) against a T0-vs-gold scoring-side macro WER of **0.5359** over 60 samples (per-sample range 0.20 to 1.33): replacing the audio with the core's OWN 53.6-percent-WER transcription costs essentially nothing at the no-supply baseline -- read honestly as a FLOOR EFFECT (at 0.174 SLU-F1 there is almost nothing left to destroy), never as 'self-transcription is lossless'. Because the two no-supply arms are equal to within 0.0001, the interaction collapses to the supply-arm modality gap (AUD-SUP - TXT-SUP = +0.0464): the modality difference is worth nothing without a roster and about +0.046 with one -- audio pays only when there is something to resolve against, which is directionally the speech-term hypothesis and still short of the registered bar. UNREGISTERED descriptive, explicitly NO clause and NO CI: the same interaction computed on slot_f1_exact is +0.1432 (Delta_AUD 0.4801 vs Delta_TXT 0.3369), pointing the same direction more strongly -- recorded because the choice of partial-credit SLU-F1 as the registered primary is now visibly consequential for any successor registration to weigh, and NOT because it changes this verdict, which it does not. **CONVERTED-SET DIFFERENCE**: aud_converted 0, txt_converted 0, aud_only (the perception-repair set) 0 -- EMPTY, and empty for a STRUCTURAL reason rather than an empirical one: the suite's conversion test requires whole-sample full correctness (exact scenario/action match AND an exact entity slot set), and intent_acc = 0 on every arm gates that conjunct to false for all 240 scored rows, which is also the origin of the 60/60 AUD-NOSUP failure count that fed the reachability statement. A NULL OF THE INSTRUMENT, NOT OF THE MECHANISM -- the slot-level metrics move +0.44 / +0.39, so supply is plainly converting slots the sample-level test cannot see. No per-sample rows exist to list and the P-PHON class cross-reference has nothing to key against; a slot-level converted-set difference would have been informative here where the sample-level one was not, recorded for a successor design. The NEAR-MISS n<=4 caveat (amendment item 3; effectively n<=3 after P-PHON disqualified its single A3 NEAR-MISS row as a clock-format variant) is carried in force and licenses no mechanism claim in any case. **INTEGRITY / VOID: not met on all six clauses** -- 3/3 silence swaps CHANGED against the unanimity bar, and diagnostically so: all three silence variants collapsed to the SAME degenerate answer (scenario 'Setting an alarm', action 'Set an alarm', roster-shaped slots wake up / five pm / eastern / morning) while their real-audio counterparts returned three completely different audio-appropriate signatures (smart-home turn off / room light; calendar Add event / baseball practice / tonight; search / restaurants / chinese / nearby), so with the roster present and the acoustics removed the core confabulates one canned answer and with the audio present it does not -- the audio genuinely drives answer content on the supply arm and the roster alone does not determine the output; 0 harness defects; 0 recheck mismatches (every row's recomputed TP/FP/FN/parsed matched its embedded metrics exactly); 0 gold-leak violations (no row's prompt contained its own sample's gold sentence -- the study's hard runtime rule verified mechanically rather than asserted); 0 transcript-reuse byte-identity mismatches (all 120 TXT-* rows sha256-matched their own T0 response); 0 duplicate-block byte-identity mismatches (all 35 byte-matched their pass-3 originals). Flight status COMPLETED, error None. **SCOPE**: descriptive, discovery-tier, n=60 SLURP devel, single run, same-distribution, within-distribution mechanism reading only; sigma_hat is measured on exactly one flight. NC CARRIED: SLURP audio CC BY-NC 4.0 on every audio-derived number. No test split was touched anywhere. No superiority, generalization, deployment, or paper-scale claim attaches. **Qualification section 2b stays OPEN and its freeze stays HELD**: section 2b made either outcome publishable only if the reachability statement licensed it, and reachability DID license a verdict -- but the verdict returned is neither a demonstrated speech term nor a CI-confirmed key-only, so neither the candidate upgrade nor the deflationary redirect of the speech-specific thesis to the meeting carrier is licensed by this read. What section 2b may now record as settled is narrower: the supply benefit is large and overwhelmingly text-side realizable, and the SLURP carrier's audio-conditioning evidence is materially stronger than the '0/6 silence-swap on three ids' that section 3 item 9 described; section 3 item 9's separate finding that the A2T carrier still has NO audio-conditioning control is untouched by this read and stands. |

The clean supply form destroys roughly 37 percent of the answers the frozen core already had right. The registration's own consequence clause applies verbatim: the signed law's negative term is real at whole-population scale and C1/C5 must carry it. **INDUCED SUBSTITUTIONS: 0 on BOTH arms** -- and this is a GENUINE EMPIRICAL ZERO ON A FIXED YARDSTICK, not an instrument null: the suite builds `supplied_values` from `full_registry_supplied_values(registry_rows)`, the FULL 152-row registry's `argument_defaults`, and applies that same yardstick to every row of both arms regardless of what each arm supplied (`registry_defaults_parse_warnings` empty, so the yardstick parsed cleanly). Had R1's damage taken the form of the core reaching for registry-default values, this counter would have caught it; it caught nothing. **So the 36 regressions are NOT value injections.** **PER-ARM** (n=97 each): R0 retention 1.0000 / reg 0 / reg_rate 0.0000 / slot F1 1.0000 / empty 0.0000 / parse-fail 0.0000 / induced 0; R1 retention 0.9278 / reg **36** / reg_rate **0.3711** / slot F1 0.7273 / empty 0.0722 / parse-fail 0.0722 / induced 0. Three counts coincide exactly at **7/97** (empty-call rate, parse-failure rate, and retention loss at 90 of 97 retained); the read does not publish the per-sample intersection so this is a strong coincidence rather than proven set equality, but the decomposition it implies is **7 samples lost tool selection** (format collapse -- nothing callable emitted) and **29 of the 90 samples that KEPT the correct tool (32 percent) still failed EM on ARGUMENTS**. The dominant failure mode is argument corruption on a correctly selected tool, exactly what a values-stripped construction would produce, and the induced-substitution yardstick confirms it is not default-copying. **PER-TIER**: R0 is uniform -- every tier 0 regressions, retention 1.0000. The damage concentrates exactly where ARGUMENTS matter -- the tiers whose gold calls require extracting specific argument values out of the speech. tier1_direct's slot-F1 column reads 0.0000 on BOTH arms and is a DEFINITIONAL ARTIFACT (zero-argument golds, qualification bound 5, carried verbatim). SIGNED SUM: cited conversions **+[8,10]/120** across three stripped renderings (**+8/120** for the B1 rendering specifically, matched to R1's construction; it is the envelope minimum, robust upward, qualification bound 2) against the **-36/97** measured here gives a net of **-26 to -28 EM samples** over the 217 defined ids (matched-rendering B1-vs-B1 net = 8 - 36 = **-28**), the same numerator over 400 with the 183-exclusion attached. **Applied blindly the clean supply form breaks between 3.6 and 4.5 answers for every one it fixes** (36/10 and 36/8). **UNLIKE the C2 conversions-vs-injections ratio that qualification bound 7 forbids as a net-utility calculation, THIS ONE IS LEGITIMATE**: both terms count the SAME unit (samples whose exact-match flips), measured with the SAME frozen scorer, on DISJOINT populations that exactly partition the defined set -- it is the first genuine signed sum this program has been able to form. Its own four binding bounds: (1) the 183 exclusion -- nearly half the frozen 400 is undefined, neither hidden upside nor hidden downside, and the account must never be stated 'over 400' without it; (2) the conversion term is CITED, not co-measured -- it comes from consumed Stage-2b reads in a different session and inherits their bounds including B1's zero-margin floor clearance; (3) the headroom set is a property of ONE A0 attempt (bound 8) and so, by the identical derivation, is the 97-id EM-correct set -- R0's 97/97 stability shows the SCORING is stable under re-fly, not that a different A0 attempt would partition the 400 the same way; (4) single run, discovery split, one carrier, and the conversion term has no measured run-to-run variance while Delta_reg does. **WHAT THIS DOES TO C1**: C1 already carried the binding disclosure that a deployment control plane cannot identify the headroom population and would supply on all 400, with the whole-population conversion-minus-regression rate UNMEASURED pending this arm. **That rate is now measured and it is negative.** (a) The CONDITIONAL claim survives intact -- on the gold-identified headroom set the values-stripped row converts [8,10]/120 and nothing here touches that; C1's mechanism content stands. (b) The UNCONDITIONAL DEPLOYMENT READING IS REFUTED -- blind application across the tool-correct population nets -26 to -28 EM samples, and this must be stated in C1 as a measured negative, not as an open item. (c) The gold-identification gap is now the whole ballgame: the measured gap between targeted supply (+8 to +10) and blind supply (-26 to -28) is **34 to 38 EM samples wide**, and closing it is an ORG/SUPPLY-side SELECTION problem (deciding WHERE to supply), not a supply-FORM problem -- every supply form tested so far is form-robust and none is safe to apply everywhere. (d) What C1 may become, with no re-cutting of any registered threshold, is a claim about supply UNDER A SELECTION RULE whose selection rule does not yet exist gold-free; that is a research object for a successor and naming it authorizes nothing. **WHAT THIS DOES TO C2**: C2's harm characterization ('rides supplied VALUES, and any in-context values, not structure') is measured here at ZERO induced substitutions on both arms while EM regression is 36/97. C2 is CORRECT about the INJECTION channel and INCOMPLETE as a statement about harm in general -- harm does not only ride supplied values. The REGRESSION channel is driven by what REPLACING supply REMOVES rather than by what it INJECTS, is roughly four times larger than the conversion benefit, and does not register on the injection yardstick at all; C2 must carry it and its 'not structure' clause must be scoped to injections. CONFOUND NAMED AND UNRESOLVED: R1 is a REPLACING supply -- the registered construction fills `{tool_schemas}` with the B1-form stripped row(s) (219-596 chars) INSTEAD OF the full 152-row registry (50,291 chars) -- so the 151 other rows disappear AND the surviving row's argument_defaults are stripped, two changes at once that THIS DESIGN CANNOT SEPARATE. Only 'removal, not injection' is licensed; whether the operative loss is the registry context or the defaults is NOT identified and must not be asserted. A successor separating stripped-augmenting from stripped-replacing is the obvious next measurement and is a fresh registration. **SYNTHESIS APPENDIX** (in the verdict document, written as the section-2b / C6 input for the qualification freeze, covering P-PHON + P-KEY + P-TRAP + V1 + V4 with every bound): the five probes converge on a candidate whose MECHANISM is better established and whose DEPLOYMENT case is worse than the draft assumed. Speech term -- matching is demonstrably acoustically mediated (P-TRAP 29/32 vs 0/32, CI [+0.8125, +0.9688]), which KILLS the deflationary 'audio is only a retrieval key' account of P-KEY's null, but leaves P-KEY's own benefit question unresolved (I = +0.0463, CI crossing zero) and the one measured instance of acoustic mediation is a COST; **section 2b stays OPEN and the freeze stays HELD**, but its statement must be REWRITTEN -- the live question is no longer 'participates or only a key' (the second disjunct is falsified) but 'participation in USE is established; its net value in the supply loop is not'. Abstention -- **C6 is NOT licensed and is FALSE as drafted**; refusal is gated by evidence quality, not the action space; C5's silence/withholding sentence is refuted in both halves; section 3 item 9 CLOSES AFFIRMATIVELY with the A2T carrier's first, decisive audio-conditioning control. Deployment -- the signed account is negative, C1's conditional claim survives and its unconditional deployment reading does not, and the decisive open problem moves from supply FORM to supply TARGETING. MAY NOT RECORD: any speech-term benefit claim, C6 in any restated form, any unconditional deployment claim for the clean form, any per-tier or per-carrier generalization, any mechanism attribution for the regression channel beyond 'removal, not injection', and anything at paper scale. **INTEGRITY**: flight status COMPLETED, split identity hash matching the frozen probe receipt, 97/97 rows per arm across 194 distinct (arm, sample_id) pairs, `registry_defaults_parse_warnings` empty, zero harness defects. **SCOPE**: descriptive, discovery-tier, n=97, single run, ONE carrier, the EM-regression-eligible population ONLY with 183 of the frozen 400 excluded by construction. NC CARRIED: Audio2Tool CC BY-NC 4.0 on every audio-derived number. No test split was touched. No superiority, generalization, deployment, or paper-scale claim attaches. |

On this carrier **refusal is gated by evidence quality, not by the action space**. **PER-CELL RATES** (n=11 each): TP-A (real audio, full 152-row registry) abstention 0 (0.0000) / confabulated call 9 (0.8182) / parse failure 2 (0.1818); TP-S (silence, full registry) abstention **11** (1.0000) / call 0 / parse failure 0; TW-A (real audio, correct tool removed) abstention 2 (0.1818) / call 5 (0.4545) / parse failure 4 (0.3636); TW-S (silence, tool removed) abstention **11** (1.0000) / call 0 / parse failure 0. Descriptive bootstrap CIs (registration section 2: published descriptively, NEVER gating the raw-count verdict; N=10,000, seed 20260807): abstention(TW-A) point 0.1818, sigma_hat 0.1169, 90 percent CI [0.0000, 0.3636]; confabulation(TP-S) point 0.0000, sigma_hat 0.0000, CI [0.0000, 0.0000]; abstention(TP-S) point 1.0000, sigma_hat 0.0000, CI [1.0000, 1.0000]. The two degenerate CIs are ARTIFACTS OF UNANIMITY, not evidence of precision -- a nonparametric bootstrap over 11 identical outcomes resamples 11 identical outcomes. Unregistered honest supplement, no clause attached: the exact one-sided 95 percent binomial lower bound for 11/11 is 0.05^(1/11) = **0.7616**, still above the 6/11 = 0.5455 floor, so ASYMMETRY-REFUTED survives the uncertainty the bootstrap cannot express. **PER-SAMPLE 4-CELL TABLE** (full table in the verdict document and `verdict.json`; every sample carries all four cells, 44 distinct (cell, sample_id) pairs, no duplicates, no unknown cells): the TP-S and TW-S columns are CONSTANT -- all 22 silence rows abstained, on every tier represented, so there is no per-sample structure on the silence side at all, which is itself the finding. On the audio side TP-A is 9 confabulated calls / 2 parse failures / 0 abstentions and TW-A is 5 calls / 4 parse failures / 2 abstentions. **MANDATORY CAVEAT -- THE FLAT DRAW IS TIER3-WEIGHTED AND THE CELL RATES ARE NOT TIER-BALANCED** (binding on all downstream citation): the registered `seeded_abst_draw` (seed 20260807, n=11) landed **6 of 11 on tier3_multi_intent** (which holds 29 of the 120 headroom ids, about 24 percent, so tier3 takes 55 percent of this draw), 2 on tier1_direct, 2 on tier8_intent_blending, 1 on tier7_multiturn, and **four of the eight A0 tiers are UNREPRESENTED**. Registered design at n=11 and disclosed rather than a defect, but no rate here may be cited as 'the A2T carrier's abstention rate'. PER-TIER BREAKDOWN: tier1_direct (n=2) TP-A 2 confab / TP-S 2 abstain / TW-A **2 abstain** / TW-S 2 abstain; tier3_multi_intent (n=6) TP-A 5 confab + 1 pfail / TP-S 6 abstain / TW-A 4 pfail + 2 confab / TW-S 6 abstain; tier7_multiturn (n=1) TP-A 1 confab / TP-S 1 abstain / TW-A 1 confab / TW-S 1 abstain; tier8_intent_blending (n=2) TP-A 1 confab + 1 pfail / TP-S 2 abstain / TW-A 2 confab / TW-S 2 abstain. **TW-A's 2/11 is ENTIRELY a tier effect** -- both abstentions are the two tier1_direct samples, **2/2 there and 0/9 everywhere else** -- so it must be read as 'abstention under tool-withholding is tier-dependent, high on tier1_direct and absent elsewhere' rather than as a scalar 0.18, and a tier1-heavy draw would have produced a much higher TW-A rate. **The VERDICT is tier-invariant**: ASYMMETRY-REFUTED fires on TP-S, which is 11/11 with ZERO variation across all four represented tiers, and ASYMMETRY-CONFIRMED could not have fired under ANY tier reweighting because its second conjunct needs confabulation(TP-S) >= 8/11 and TP-S produced zero calls on every tier. The caveat constrains the magnitudes and does not touch the verdict. **C6 LICENSING STATEMENT (registration section 2, verbatim in force)**: the registration binds the C6 licence solely to ASYMMETRY-CONFIRMED, which did not fire and failed on both conjuncts, so **C6 is NOT LICENSED at discovery tier or any other tier**. Stronger than unproven: this read measures C6's content and finds it FALSE on this carrier -- C6 asserted refusal gated by the action space, and refusal is gated by evidence quality (11/11 abstention under silence on BOTH registry conditions) and not by the action space (2/11 under tool-withholding). C6 must not be restated, softened or re-scoped into the claim set on the strength of this read; a successor safety claim needs a fresh registration matching the measured direction and its own owner decision. **SECTION 3 ITEM 9 IS CLOSED AFFIRMATIVELY** (the half of the registration's purpose that succeeds independently of the verdict branch): the audio2tool carrier -- C1's primary-evidence carrier -- now has a within-carrier audio-conditioning control and it is DECISIVE rather than suggestive. Holding the registry fixed at its full 152 rows and changing only the audio to a duration-matched silence swap moves the core from **9/11 confabulated calls and 0/11 abstentions to 0/11 calls and 11/11 abstentions**. Where item 9 recorded 'no audio-conditioning control exists on the A2T carrier' (its prior basis being silence-swap 0/6 on three SLURP devel ids flown twice), the successor record is 'measured, within-carrier, n=11, tier3-weighted: tool-calling on this carrier is gated by audio presence'. **CONSEQUENCES FOR THE CLAIM SET, as CORRECTIONS not additions**: (a) **C5 must be corrected**. C5 reads 'Under silence the core confabulates confident well-formed commands while under tool-withholding it abstains cleanly -- the refusal-is-gated-by-action-space asymmetry is recorded as an OPEN safety question (promotable to a claim only via the registered within-carrier 2x2)'. The 2x2 has now run and BOTH HALVES of that sentence are refuted on the A2T carrier: under silence the core abstains cleanly (11/11, zero calls) and under tool-withholding it does not abstain (2/11). The open safety question is not promoted; it is CLOSED AGAINST the drafted direction and C5's sentence must be rewritten to the measured facts. (b) The SLURP silence-confabulation observation (P-KEY: roster present, audio removed, 3/3 collapse to one canned roster-populated answer) is untouched on SLURP but **does not transfer to A2T** -- the two carriers behave oppositely under the same manipulation, exactly as the REFUTED branch's registered wording anticipated. A READING and not a measurement for why: SLURP's answer schema always expects a filled slot structure so 'nothing to say' has no legal surface form, while A2T's tool-calling protocol has a native legal empty output and the core uses it; whether the governing variable is the output format's admission of refusal is a testable successor question and is NOT tested here. (c) **Structural withholding from a FULL registry produces wrong actions, not refusals** -- only 2 of 11 handled the missing action correctly while 9 of 11 produced a wrong call or unparseable output. This does NOT overturn the consumed S4 result (retention 0.0000, 111/120 well-formed abstentions): S4 REPLACED the roster with a narrow wrong set, whereas TW-A removes one tool from an otherwise intact 152-row registry leaving about 151 plausible alternatives in view. The two constructions behave oppositely and the difference is the presence of alternatives -- narrowing a roster fails to SILENCE, deleting one row from a full roster fails to SUBSTITUTION. TW-A is NOT a re-measurement of S4 and must not be cited as one. (d) C3's format-collapse line is corroborated: TW-A's parse failures (4/11, ALL on tier3_multi_intent) double TP-A's (2/11) under a registry manipulation. **INTEGRITY**: flight status COMPLETED, split identity hash matching the frozen probe receipt, 11/11/11/11 rows across the four cells, 44 distinct (cell, sample_id) pairs, no duplicate pairs, no unknown-cell rows, all four cells sharing the IDENTICAL sample-id set, zero harness defects after the receipt-key repair. **SCOPE**: descriptive, discovery-tier, n=11 per cell, single run, ONE carrier, a tier3-weighted flat draw with four of eight tiers unrepresented. NC CARRIED: Audio2Tool CC BY-NC 4.0 on every audio-derived number. No test split was touched. No superiority, generalization, deployment, or paper-scale claim attaches. |

Any successor must treat a +0.10 floor against a ~0.82 baseline as near-degenerate and enlarge n, restrict to the hard stratum, or re-register the floor. The FS-INERT equivalence branch was equally live before the data was opened. **VERDICT: INCONCLUSIVE-FEW** -- no registered branch fires. ACCURACIES (closed-set scenario vs gold, scoring-side): FS-NONE **49/60 = 0.8167**, FS-BAL **49/60 = 0.8167**, FS-SKEW **45/60 = 0.7500**. CONTRASTS (paired bootstrap, shared `analysis/bootstrap.py`, N=10,000 replicates, seed 20260807): BAL-NONE point **+0.0000**, sigma_hat 0.0411, 90 percent CI **[-0.0667, +0.0667]**; NONE-SKEW point **+0.0667**, sigma_hat 0.0323, 90 percent CI **[+0.0167, +0.1167]**. CLAUSE MARGINS: FS-HELPS point (>= +0.10) NOT MET, short by **0.1000** = 6 samples; FS-HELPS CI-gated NOT MET -- failed on the point estimate, CI low -0.0667 sits **0.0667 below** the 0 gate; FS-INERT point (abs <= 0.05) **MET** at an exact zero, clearing by the maximum possible **0.0500**; FS-INERT CI-gated (authoritative) **NOT MET** -- the CI [-0.0667, +0.0667] overhangs the +/-0.05 band by **0.0167 at each edge, one sample of resolution out of sixty**, and the suite's own output states the rule verbatim ("a point estimate inside the band with a CI crossing it is INCONCLUSIVE-FEW, never an affirmative FS-INERT"); BIAS-SENSITIVE overlay NOT MET on BOTH conjuncts -- degradation +0.0667 vs floor +0.15 short by **0.0833** = 5 samples, capture rate 0.0339 vs floor 0.25 short by **0.2161**. **The FS-INERT near-miss is an instrument limit, not a phenomenon**: at n=60 the paired bootstrap cannot resolve a +/-0.05 equivalence band, so the defensible reading is "no gain was detected AND this design cannot certify the absence of one at +/-0.05", never "few-shot is inert". MAPPING OUTCOMES: **`{exact: 60}` on all three arms, 180/180 non-fallback** -- no case, substring or unmapped tier on any row. This is the second independent confirmation of the closed-set output contract after A1'r, and it extends it: the contract survives a prepended four-demo block and survives a maximally skewed one, so the ENTIRE FS-SKEW loss is wrong-label choice and none of it is malformed output. `harness_defect_reasons` empty; flight status COMPLETED with error null; 60/60 rows per arm and 180 distinct (arm, sample_id) pairs; `runtime_prohibited=true` and the suite's own note ("gold-derived; scoring-side only; must never enter a runtime path") travel in verdict.json. **SKEW-CAPTURE ACCOUNTING (the disclosed denominator, now measured for the first time)**: the seeded GLOBAL skew label was **`audio`** (drawn once with zero gold input, because a per-sample "wrong for THIS sample" choice would need that sample's own devel gold at binding time and the research boundary forbids it); exactly **1 of 60** samples has gold == `audio` (`slurp:devel:000115:audio-1495375974`) and is excluded as undefined, giving denominator **59**; capture count **2**, rate **2/59 = 0.0339**. **The 0.0339 is not a small capture, it is NO capture**: FS-SKEW predicted `audio` on exactly three rows and all three are rows the ZERO-DEMO arm already answered `audio` on unprompted (`000115` gold=audio excluded, `009340` gold=play, `011503` gold=play) -- so **incremental capture attributable to the 240 skewed demo slots is 0/59 = 0.0000**, and the marginal says the same without reference to any row: `audio` is predicted **3 times under FS-NONE, 3 times under FS-BAL, 3 times under FS-SKEW**. Four demonstrations of a label in every one of 60 prompts moved that label's predicted marginal by ZERO. **The degradation is real but is NOT prior-parroting**: FS-SKEW loses 4 and gains 0 against FS-NONE (strictly one-directional), and **none of the four losses went to `audio`** -- two went to `general`, the catch-all, whose marginal rises 1 -> 2 -> 3 across NONE/BAL/SKEW (`005359` datetime->general, `008072` calendar->datetime, `009733` play->general, `011654` recommendation->transport). A maximally skewed four-shot prior therefore costs about 4 samples in 60 (CI above zero) and buys the skewed label nothing: **the classic ICL label-prior-bias failure mode did NOT reproduce; a smaller generic ICL-noise failure mode did.** This is the label-level companion to P-TRAP's 0/32 cross-sample content-level control -- two independent probes, two exact zeros, on the registration's founding premise that the word-level resonance law does not extend to the label level. **PER-SAMPLE TABLE**: the full 60-row three-arm table (sample_id, gold, per-arm label and correctness) is `per_sample` in `docs/checks/speech-aware-evidence-acquisition/2026-08-19-pfew-read/verdict.json` and is rendered verbatim in that directory's `report.txt`; it is not duplicated in the ledger. Two structural readings drawn from it. (i) **The primary null is a WASH, not stillness**: FS-BAL and FS-NONE tie at 49 but agree on only 54 of 60 labels, differing on 6 with an exact **3 gains / 3 losses** split (gains `005025` music->iot, `008047` lists->calendar, `009518` news->play; losses `000943` datetime->qa, `009733` play->general, `012478` transport->takeaway); balanced demos move 10 percent of the population and move it in both directions equally, acting as a RE-RANDOMIZATION of borderline decisions rather than as information about the label space (all three arms identical on 51/60). (ii) **The residual error is one confusable cluster, not diffuse noise**: `play` accounts for **6 of the 11** zero-demo errors and collapses into its media neighbours (3 -> `music`, 2 -> `audio`, 1 -> `news`), scoring 1/7 on FS-NONE, 1/7 on FS-BAL and 0/7 on FS-SKEW, while **9 of the 18 canonical classes are perfect on all three arms**; more than half the available headroom therefore sits in a single systematic confusion that generic demonstrations provably cannot address, which is the concrete reason the +0.10 gain was never in reach. **UNREGISTERED DESCRIPTIVE, NO CLAUSE ATTACHED -- IN-SESSION REPLICATION DISCREPANCY**: the in-session FS-NONE comparator reads **49/60 = 0.8167** where the cited consumed A1'r phase-1 read **47/60 = 0.7833** on the SAME 60 samples, with a pre-flight-verified BYTE-IDENTICAL prompt block (`pslu-s2-a1r-phase1-closed-set-v1`, 432 chars), the same decode config (`-c 49152 -np 4 -fa on -ngl 999 -ctk q8_0 -ctv q8_0`; temperature 0, seed 20260803, max_tokens 1024), the same split identity hash and the same runtime build and GGUF hashes. Candidate explanations this read cannot adjudicate (and no further model contact is authorized to try): continuous-batching nondeterminism under `-np 4` (slot assignment and batch composition differ between a 183-row four-arm session and a 180-row three-arm session, changing floating-point reduction order; temperature 0 fixes the sampling rule, not the logits), and session/context differences (A1'r phase-1 was interleaved with three other request families, FS-NONE was a contiguous 60-row block). **The magnitude is what matters: a nominally deterministic configuration reproduced to +/-2 samples = +/-0.0333 across sessions -- LARGER than the entire BAL-NONE contrast this probe was built to measure and two-thirds of the +/-0.05 equivalence band FS-INERT was gated on.** Consequences carried forward: the registration's choice to fly an in-session FS-NONE rather than cite 47/60 was correct and every future routing-tier contrast on this carrier must do the same; the scheme scoreboard should read A1'r closed-set gold agreement as **0.78-0.82 across two sessions**, not as the point value 0.7833; and any successor equivalence band at +/-0.05 on n=60 is now known to sit INSIDE cross-session reproduction noise independent of bootstrap width. **WHAT IT ADDS TO THE ROUTING-TIER HYPOTHESIS** (scheme scoreboard `docs/readiness/2026-08-19-scheme-scoreboard.md`, current best-scheme statement v1, routing tier; cited context A1'r 60/60 non-fallback and 47/60 gold agreement): P-FEW resolves the BIAS half of the "few-shot routing gain/bias" ledger row and returns a hard null on the GAIN half. Scoreboard deltas this read licenses, to be applied when the arc's reads are consolidated -- "few-shot routing gain" moves from under-test to **NEGATIVE 0/1** (BAL-NONE +0.0000, CI [-0.0667, +0.0667]; equivalence NOT certified because the CI is wider than +/-0.05); "few-shot routing bias / label-prior capture" moves to **DOES NOT OCCUR** (0/59 incremental capture, skew-label marginal unchanged 3->3->3, with a small non-capture ICL cost of +0.0667 whose CI lies above zero); "closed-set decode routing viable" moves to **2/2** with the caveat that its gold agreement is a 0.78-0.82 range across sessions rather than a point value; and a new row "label-level supply has no resonance channel" enters at **2/2** (P-TRAP 0/32 content, P-FEW 0/59 label). The tier's improvement path is thereby narrowed to (a) a DIFFERENT REPRESENTATION -- embedding matching, P-EMB-1/2, read next -- rather than more prompt context, or (b) TARGETED corrective supply aimed at the measured confusion neighbourhood, the routing-tier twin of the filling tier's already-registered supply-targeting problem and the natural object of a P-CORR-style design; "more demonstrations" is eliminated as a lever at K=4 on this carrier. **SCOPE**: descriptive, discovery-tier, n=60 SLURP devel, ONE run per arm, same-distribution, one carrier, one core, K=4 only, ONE seeded skew label; sigma_hat is measured on exactly one flight and cross-session reproduction on this instrument is +/-2 samples. NC CARRIED: SLURP audio CC BY-NC 4.0 on every audio-derived number. Gold labels were used scoring-side only and never entered a runtime path; demo content was TRAIN-split only under the binding-time fail-closed devel-exclusion check. No test split was touched. No superiority, generalization, deployment, or paper-scale claim attaches. |
